    SELECT [o].[Quantity], [o].[UnitPrice], [o].[UnitPriceWithDiscount], DATEPART(hour, [o0].[CreatedAt]) AS [Key]
    FROM [OrderItems] AS [o]
    INNER JOIN [Orders] AS [o0] ON [o].[OrderId] = [o0].[OrderId]
    WHERE [o0].[RestaurantId] = @__restaurantId_0 AND [o0].[CreatedAt] >= @__yesterdayStart_1 AND [o0].[CreatedAt] < @__todayStart_2
) AS [s]
GROUP BY [s].[Key]
